# SEO Tool Review Queue

This file records human approval state for the one-page-at-a-time SEO review lane. The full generated queue is produced by `npm run aft -- seo-tool-queue` and saved under `output/seo-tool-review/queue/`.

Do not mark a page `approved` from a score alone. Approval means the user has reviewed the exact page, browser proof, report paths, and diff for that tool page or blog page.
